        return !this._resolved && !this.hasAttemptedResolve && AS(),

        },

    }
    onPostResolved() {}

        ...s
    }));
    return n.displayName = wp(e),
    n
}

function Z(e) {
    return Number.isFinite(e) ? Math.floor(e).toLocaleString("en-US") : "0"
}
function wj(e) {
    const t = new Date(e);
    if (isNaN(t.getTime()))
        return "--";
    const n = String(t.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")
      , r = String(t.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${n}-${r}`
}
function ol(e) {
    const t = new Date(e);
    if (isNaN(t.getTime()))
        return "--:--";

function d5(e) {
    const [t,n] = F.useState("")
      , r = F.useRef(null);
    return F.useEffect(()=>{
        const s = ()=>{
            const i = new Date(e).getTime() - Date.now();
            if (i <= 0) {
                n("00:00:00"),
                r.current && clearInterval(r.current);
                return
            }
            const o = String(Math.floor(i / 36e5)).padStart(2, "0")
              , a = String(Math.floor(i % 36e5 / 6e4)).padStart(2, "0")
              , l = String(Math.floor(i % 6e4 / 1e3)).padStart(2, "0");
            n(`${o}:${a}:${l}`)
        }
        ;
        return s(),
        r.current = setInterval(s, 1e3),
        ()=>{
            r.current && clearInterval(r.current)
        }
    }
    , [e]),
    t
}
function f5(e, t) {
    const n = new Date;
    return Array.from({
        length: e
    }, (r,s)=>{
        const i = new Date(n);
        i.setDate(i.getDate() - (e - 1 - s));
        const o = i.toISOString().split("T")[0];
        return {
            date: `${i.getMonth() + 1}.${i.getDate()}`,
            done: t.includes(o)
        }
    }
    )
}

function M5(e) {
    const t = new Map;
    for (const s of e) {
        const i = s.createTime.split(" ")[0] || s.createTime.slice(0, 10)
          , o = t.get(i);
        o ? (s.amount > 0 ? o.income += s.amount : o.expense += Math.abs(s.amount),
        o.items.push(s)) : t.set(i, {
            income: s.amount > 0 ? s.amount : 0,
            expense: s.amount < 0 ? Math.abs(s.amount) : 0,
            items: [s]
        })
    }
    const n = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
      , r = new Date(Date.now() - 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    return Array.from(t.entries()).map(([s,i])=>{
        const o = s.slice(5).replace("-", ".");
        return {
            dateLabel: s === n ? "今天" : s === r ? "昨天" : o,
            dateSuffix: o,
            income: i.income,
            expense: i.expense,
            transactions: i.items
        }
    }
    )
}

function q5(e) {
    const {sessionId: t, onMessage: n, onOpen: r, onClose: s, enabled: i=!0} = e
      , o = F.useRef(null)
      , a = F.useRef(0)
      , l = F.useRef(null)
      , [u,d] = F.useState(!1)
      , f = F.useRef(n);
    f.current = n;

    h.current = r;
    const w = F.useRef(s);
    w.current = s;
    const p = F.useCallback(()=>{
        l.current && (clearTimeout(l.current),
        l.current = null),
        o.current && (o.current.close(),
        o.current = null),
        d(!1)
    }
    , [])
      , x = F.useCallback(()=>{
        if (!i || !t)
            return;
        o.current && (o.current.close(),
        o.current = null),
        l.current && (clearTimeout(l.current),
        l.current = null);
        const m = `wss://micro-life.iwhop.cn/spark/ws/rain/${t}`
          , g = new WebSocket(m);
        g.onopen = ()=>{
            var y;
            a.current = 0,
            d(!0),
            (y = h.current) == null || y.call(h)
        }
        ,
        g.onmessage = y=>{
            try {
                const b = JSON.parse(y.data);
                f.current(b)
            } catch {}
        }
        ,
        g.onclose = ()=>{
            var y;
            if (d(!1),
            (y = w.current) == null || y.call(w),
            a.current < H5 && i) {
                const b = K5 * Math.pow(2, a.current);
                a.current += 1,
                l.current = setTimeout(x, b)
            }
        }
        ,

            g.close()
        }
        ,
        o.current = g
    }
    , [t, i]);
    F.useEffect(()=>(x(),
    p), [x, p]);

        var g;
        ((g = o.current) == null ? void 0 : g.readyState) === WebSocket.OPEN && o.current.send(JSON.stringify(m))

    }
}

function mx(e) {

      , r = n.display;

        l.dataset.polyfillMt = i) : l.dataset.polyfillMl !== i && (l.style.marginLeft = i,

    }

}
